{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/ac-branch-pi-model\n\n# AC Branch Pi-Model + Transformer Handling\n\nImplement the exact branch power flow equations in `acopf-math-model.md` using MATPOWER branch data:\n\n```\n[F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, TAP, SHIFT, BR_STATUS, ANGMIN, ANGMAX]\n```\n\n## Quick start\n\n- Use `scripts/branch_flows.py` to compute per-unit branch flows.\n- Treat the results as **power leaving** the “from” bus and **power leaving** the “to” bus (i.e., compute both directions explicitly).\n\nExample:\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom scripts.branch_flows import compute_branch_flows_pu, build_bus_id_to_idx\n\ndata = json.load(open(\"/root/network.json\"))\nbaseMVA = float(data[\"baseMVA\"])\nbuses = np.array(data[\"bus\"], dtype=float)\nbranches = np.array(data[\"branch\"], dtype=float)\n\nbus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(buses)\n\nVm = buses[:, 7]  # initial guess VM\nVa = np.deg2rad(buses[:, 8])  # initial guess VA\n\nbr = branches[0]\nP_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = compute_branch_flows_pu(Vm, Va, br, bus_id_to_idx)\n\nS_ij_MVA = (P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nS_ji_MVA = (P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nprint(S_ij_MVA, S_ji_MVA)\n```\n\n## Model details (match the task formulation)\n\n### Per-unit conventions\n\n- Work in per-unit internally.\n- Convert with `baseMVA`:\n  - \\(P_{pu} = P_{MW} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(Q_{pu} = Q_{MVAr} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(|S|_{MVA} = |S|_{pu} \\cdot baseMVA\\)\n\n### Transformer handling (MATPOWER TAP + SHIFT)\n\n- Use \\(T_{ij} = tap \\cdot e^{j \\cdot shift}\\).\n- **Implementation shortcut (real tap + phase shift):**\n  - If `abs(TAP) < 1e-12`, treat `tap = 1.0` (no transformer).\n  - Convert `SHIFT` from degrees to radians.\n  - Use the angle shift by modifying the angle difference:\n    - \\(\\delta_{ij} = \\theta_i - \\theta_j - shift\\)\n    - \\(\\delta_{ji} = \\theta_j - \\theta_i + shift\\)\n\n### Series admittance\n\nGiven `BR_R = r`, `BR_X = x`:\n\n- If `r == 0 and x == 0`, set `g = 0`, `b = 0` (avoid divide-by-zero).\n- Else:\n  - \\(y = 1/(r + jx) = g + jb\\)\n  - \\(g = r/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n  - \\(b = -x/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n\n### Line charging susceptance\n\n- `BR_B` is the **total** line charging susceptance \\(b_c\\) (per unit).\n- Each end gets \\(b_c/2\\) in the standard pi model.\n\n## Power flow equations (use these exactly)\n\nLet:\n- \\(V_i = |V_i| e^{j\\theta_i}\\), \\(V_j = |V_j| e^{j\\theta_j}\\)\n- `tap` is real, `shift` is radians\n- `inv_t = 1/tap`, `inv_t2 = inv_t^2`\n\nThen the real/reactive power flow from i→j is:\n\n- \\(P_{ij} = g |V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ij} + b\\sin\\delta_{ij})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ij} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ij} - b\\cos\\delta_{ij})\\)\n\nAnd from j→i is:\n\n- \\(P_{ji} = g |V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ji} + b\\sin\\delta_{ji})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ji} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ji} - b\\cos\\delta_{ji})\\)\n\nCompute apparent power:\n\n- \\(|S_{ij}| = \\sqrt{P_{ij}^2 + Q_{ij}^2}\\)\n- \\(|S_{ji}| = \\sqrt{P_{ji}^2 + Q_{ji}^2}\\)\n\n## Common uses\n\n### Enforce MVA limits (rateA)\n\n- `RATE_A` is an MVA limit (may be 0 meaning “no limit”).\n- Enforce in both directions:\n  - \\(|S_{ij}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n  - \\(|S_{ji}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n\n### Compute branch loading %\n\nFor reporting “most loaded branches”:\n\n- `loading_pct = 100 * max(|S_ij|, |S_ji|) / RATE_A` if `RATE_A > 0`, else 0.\n\n### Aggregate bus injections for nodal balance\n\nTo build the branch flow sum for each bus \\(i\\):\n\n- Add \\(P_{ij}, Q_{ij}\\) to bus i\n- Add \\(P_{ji}, Q_{ji}\\) to bus j\n\nThis yields arrays `P_out[i]`, `Q_out[i]` such that the nodal balance can be written as:\n\n- \\(P^g - P^d - G^s|V|^2 = P_{out}\\)\n- \\(Q^g - Q^d + B^s|V|^2 = Q_{out}\\)\n\n## Sanity checks (fast debug)\n\n- With `SHIFT=0` and `TAP=1`, if \\(V_i = V_j\\) and \\(\\theta_i=\\theta_j\\), then \\(P_{ij}\\approx 0\\) and \\(P_{ji}\\approx 0\\) (lossless only if r=0).\n- For a pure transformer (`r=x=0`) you should not get meaningful flows; treat as `g=b=0` (no series element).\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b9cef13c-20b3-4dc3-abfb-10d47633de50","uuid":"073648e8-3713-4a76-9daf-399a4abcbb14","isSynthetic":true}

Load network data\n        with open(network_file) as f:\n            self.data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = float(self.data.get('baseMVA', base_mva))\n\n        # Parse network components\n        self.buses = np.array(self.data['bus'], dtype=float)\n        self.generators = np.array(self.data['gen'], dtype=float)\n        self.branches = np.array(self.data['branch'], dtype=float)\n        self.gencost = np.array(self.data.get('gencost', []), dtype=float)\n\n        self.n_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.n_gen = len(self.generators)\n        self.n_branch = len(self.branches)\n\n        # Build bus index mapping\n        self.bus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(self.buses)\n\n        # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format columns)\n        # [BUS_I, TYPE, PD, QD, GS, BS, BUS_AREA, VM, VA, BASE_KV, ZONE, VMAX, VMIN]\n        self.bus_ids = self.buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.bus_type = self.buses[:, 1].astype(int)\n        self.p_demand = self.buses[:, 2] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.q_demand = self.buses[:, 3] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.gs_shunt = self.buses[:, 4] / self.base_mva\n        self.bs_shunt = self.buses[:, 5] / self.base_mva\n        self.vm_init = self.buses[:, 7]\n        self.va_init = np.deg2rad(self.buses[:, 8])\n        self.vmax = self.buses[:, 11]\n        self.vmin = self.buses[:, 12]\n\n        # pu\n        self.tap = self.branches[:, 8]\n        self.shift = np.deg2rad(self.branches[:, 9])\n        self.br_status = self.branches[:, 10]\n        self.angmin = np.deg2rad(self.branches[:, 11])\n        self.angmax = np.deg2rad(self.branches[:, 12])\n\n        # Compute series admittances\n        self._compute_series_admittances()\n\n        # Solution storage\n        self.solution = None\n        self.solver_status = None\n\n    def _compute_series_admittances(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute series admittances for all branches\"\"\"\n        self.br_g = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        self.br_b = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for i in range(self.n_branch):\n            r = self.br_r[i]\n            x = self.br_x[i]\n\n            if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x) < 1e-12:\n                self.br_g[i] = 0.0\n                self.br_b[i] = 0.0\n            else:\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                self.br_g[i] = r / z_sq\n                self.br_b[i] = -x / z_sq\n\n    def _compute_branch_flows(self, vm: np.ndarray, va: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute apparent power flows for all branches in MW and MVAr\n        Returns: (pij_mw, qij_mvar, pji_mw, qji_mvar, sij_mva, sji_mva)\n        \"\"\"\n        pij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        qij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        pji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        qji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        sij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        sji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for k in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[k] < 0.5:\n                continue\n\n            # Get bus indices\n            i = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[k])]\n            j = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[k])]\n\n            # Branch parameters\n            g = self.br_g[k]\n            b = self.br_b[k]\n            bc = self.br_b[k]  # charging susceptance (total)\n            tap = self.tap[k] if abs(self.tap[k]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = self.shift[k]\n\n            # Voltage magnitudes and angles\n            vm_i = vm[i]\n            vm_j = vm[j]\n            va_i = va[i]\n            va_j = va[j]\n\n            # Angle differences\n            delta_ij = va_i - va_j - shift\n            delta_ji = va_j - va_i + shift\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            # Power flows (from AC branch pi-model)\n            cos_dij = np.cos(delta_ij)\n            sin_dij = np.sin(delta_ij)\n            cos_dji = np.cos(delta_ji)\n            sin_dji = np.sin(delta_ji)\n\n            # i -> j\n            pij[k] = g * vm_i**2 * inv_t2 - vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * cos_dij + b * sin_dij)\n            qij[k] = -(b + bc/2) * vm_i**2 * inv_t2 - vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * sin_dij - b * cos_dij)\n\n            # j -> i\n            pji[k] = g * vm_j**2 - vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * cos_dji + b * sin_dji)\n            qji[k] = -(b + bc/2) * vm_j**2 - vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * sin_dji - b * cos_dji)\n\n            # Apparent power\n            sij[k] = np.sqrt(pij[k]**2 + qij[k]**2) * self.base_mva\n            sji[k] = np.sqrt(pji[k]**2 + qji[k]**2) * self.base_mva\n\n        return pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji\n\n    def solve(self, verbose: bool = False) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve the AC-OPF problem\n        Returns True if optimal solution found\n        \"\"\"\n        # Decision variables\n        vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', self.n_buses)  # voltage magnitudes\n        va = ca.SX.sym('va', self.n_buses)  # voltage angles\n        pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', self.n_gen)    # generator real power\n        qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', self.n_gen)    # generator reactive power\n\n        # Concatenate all variables\n        variables = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Build cost function\n        cost = 0\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if len(self.gencost) > k:\n                # gencost format: [TYPE, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, N, COEFF_1, COEFF_2, ...]\n                coeff = self.gencost[k]\n                if coeff[0] == 2:  # polynomial cost\n                    n = int(coeff[3])\n                    # Quadratic cost: c2*pg^2 + c1*pg + c0\n                    if n >= 1:\n                        c0 = coeff[4 + n - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 1 else 0\n                        c1 = coeff[4 + n - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 2 else 0\n                        c2 = coeff[4 + n - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 3 else 0\n                        cost += c2 * pg[k]**2 + c1 * pg[k] + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        constraints = []\n        constraint_lbs = []\n        constraint_ubs = []\n\n        # 1. Voltage angle at reference buses (slack bus at bus 0)\n        constraints.append(va[0])  # va[0] = 0\n        constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n        constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n        # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            constraints.append(vm[i])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.vmin[i])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.vmax[i])\n\n        # 3. Generator real power bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(pg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.pg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.pg_max[k])\n\n        # 4. Generator reactive power bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(qg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.qg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.qg_max[k])\n\n        # 5. Power flow balance equations\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            # Real power balance\n            p_gen = 0\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i:  # account for 1-based bus indexing\n                    p_gen = p_gen + pg[k]\n\n            # Compute power flows at this bus (sum of flows to adjacent buses)\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5:\n                    continue\n\n                from_bus = int(self.br_from[br_idx]) - 1\n                to_bus = int(self.br_to[br_idx]) - 1\n\n                g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n                b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n                shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n                inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n                inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n                if from_bus == i:\n                    # Power leaving bus i to bus j\n                    delta_ij = va[i] - va[to_bus] - shift\n                    cos_dij = ca.cos(delta_ij)\n                    sin_dij = ca.sin(delta_ij)\n\n                    p_flow = p_flow + (g * vm[i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                                      vm[i] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * cos_dij + b * sin_dij))\n                    q_flow = q_flow + (-(b + bc/2) * vm[i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                                      vm[i] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * sin_dij - b * cos_dij))\n\n                elif to_bus == i:\n                    # Power leaving bus i to bus from_bus\n                    delta_ji = va[i] - va[from_bus] + shift\n                    cos_dji = ca.cos(delta_ji)\n                    sin_dji = ca.sin(delta_ji)\n\n                    p_flow = p_flow + (g * vm[i]**2 -\n                                      vm[i] * vm[from_bus] * inv_t * (g * cos_dji + b * sin_dji))\n                    q_flow = q_flow + (-(b + bc/2) * vm[i]**2 -\n                                      vm[i] * vm[from_bus] * inv_t * (g * sin_dji - b * cos_dji))\n\n            # Real power balance: Pg - Pd - Gs*Vm^2 = P_flow\n            p_balance = p_gen - self.p_demand[i] - self.gs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - p_flow\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n            # Reactive power balance: Qg - Qd + Bs*Vm^2 = Q_flow\n            q_balance = 0\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i:\n                    q_balance = q_balance + qg[k]\n\n            q_balance = q_balance - self.q_demand[i] + self.bs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - q_flow\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n        # 6. Branch thermal limits (apparent power)\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5 or self.rate_a[br_idx] < 1e-6:\n                continue\n\n            from_bus = int(self.br_from[br_idx]) - 1\n            to_bus = int(self.br_to[br_idx]) - 1\n\n            g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n            b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            # i -> j\n            delta_ij = va[from_bus] - va[to_bus] - shift\n            cos_dij = ca.cos(delta_ij)\n            sin_dij = ca.sin(delta_ij)\n\n            p_ij = (g * vm[from_bus]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                   vm[from_bus] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * cos_dij + b * sin_dij))\n            q_ij = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[from_bus]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                   vm[from_bus] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * sin_dij - b * cos_dij))\n            s_ij = ca.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n\n            constraints.append(s_ij)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx])\n\n            # j -> i\n            delta_ji = va[to_bus] - va[from_bus] + shift\n            cos_dji = ca.cos(delta_ji)\n            sin_dji = ca.sin(delta_ji)\n\n            p_ji = (g * vm[to_bus]**2 -\n                   vm[from_bus] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * cos_dji + b * sin_dji))\n            q_ji = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[to_bus]**2 -\n                   vm[from_bus] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * sin_dji - b * cos_dji))\n            s_ji = ca.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            constraints.append(s_ji)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx])\n\n        # Build NLP\n        nlp = {\n            'x': variables,\n            'f': cost,\n            'g': ca.vertcat(*constraints) if constraints else ca.DM([])\n        }\n\n        # Create solver\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 5 if verbose else 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 1000,\n            'print_time': verbose,\n        }\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.concatenate([\n            self.vm_init,\n            self.va_init,\n            self.pg_init,\n            self.qg_init\n        ])\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = np.concatenate([\n            self.vmin,\n            -np.pi * np.ones(self.n_buses),\n            self.pg_min,\n            self.qg_min\n        ])\n\n        ubx = np.concatenate([\n            self.vmax,\n            np.pi * np.ones(self.n_buses),\n            self.pg_max,\n            self.qg_max\n        ])\n\n        # Solve\n        result = solver(\n            x0=x0,\n            lbx=lbx,\n            ubx=ubx,\n            lbg=np.array(constraint_lbs),\n            ubg=np.array(constraint_ubs)\n        )\n\n        # Extract solution\n        sol = result['x'].full().flatten()\n        self.vm_sol = sol[:self.n_buses]\n        self.va_sol = sol[self.n_buses:2*self.n_buses]\n        self.pg_sol = sol[2*self.n_buses:2*self.n_buses + self.n_gen]\n        self.qg_sol = sol[2*self.n_buses + self.n_gen:]\n\n        # Check solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        self.solver_status = stats['return_status']\n\n        if verbose:\n            print(f\"Solver status: {self.solver_status}\")\n\n        return self.solver_status == 'Solve_Succeeded'\n\n    def generate_report(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n        if self.vm_sol is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Solver has not been run successfully\")\n\n        # Compute final branch flows\n        pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji = self._compute_branch_flows(self.vm_sol, self.va_sol)\n\n        # Convert to MW and MVAr\n        pg_mw = self.pg_sol * self.base_mva\n        qg_mvar = self.qg_sol * self.base_mva\n        sij_mva = sij\n        sji_mva = sji\n\n        # np.sum(self.q_demand) * self.base_mva\n\n        # Calculate losses\n        total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n        # Calculate cost\n        total_cost = 0\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if len(self.gencost) > k:\n                coeff = self.gencost[k]\n                if coeff[0] == 2:\n                    n = int(coeff[3])\n                    if n >= 1:\n                        c0 = coeff[4 + n - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 1 else 0\n                        c1 = coeff[4 + n - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 2 else 0\n                        c2 = coeff[4 + n - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 3 else 0\n                        total_cost += c2 * self.pg_sol[k]**2 + c1 * self.pg_sol[k] + c0\n                        total_cost = float(total_cost) * self.base_mva\n\n        # Build generators section\n        generators = []\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            gen_bus_idx = int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1\n            generators.append({\n                \"id\": k + 1,\n                \"bus\": int(self.gen_bus[k]),\n                \"pg_MW\": float(pg_mw[k]),\n                \"qg_MVAr\": float(qg_mvar[k]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(self.pg_min[k] * self.base_mva),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(self.pg_max[k] * self.base_mva),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(self.qg_min[k] * self.base_mva),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(self.qg_max[k] * self.base_mva)\n            })\n\n        # Build buses section\n        buses = []\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            buses.append({\n                \"id\": int(self.bus_ids[i]),\n                \"vm_pu\": float(self.vm_sol[i]),\n                \"va_deg\": float(np.rad2deg(self.va_sol[i])),\n                \"vmin_pu\": float(self.vmin[i]),\n                \"vmax_pu\": float(self.vmax[i])\n            })\n\n        # Find most loaded branches\n        branch_loadings = []\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5 or self.rate_a[br_idx] < 1e-6:\n                continue\n\n            max_flow = max(sij_mva[br_idx], sji_mva[br_idx])\n            loading_pct = 100.0 * max_flow / (self.rate_a[br_idx] * self.base_mva)\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                \"from_bus\": int(self.br_from[br_idx]),\n                \"to_bus\": int(self.br_to[br_idx]),\n                \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n                \"flow_from_MVA\": float(sij_mva[br_idx]),\n                \"flow_to_MVA\": float(sji_mva[br_idx]),\n                \"limit_MVA\": float(self.rate_a[br_idx] * self.base_mva),\n                \"branch_idx\": br_idx\n            })\n\n        # Sort by loading and get top 10\n        branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n        most_loaded = [b for b in branch_loadings[:10]]\n        for b in most_loaded:\n            del b['branch_idx']\n\n        # Feasibility check\n        max_p_mismatch = 0\n        max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            p_gen = 0\n            q_gen = 0\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i:\n                    p_gen += self.pg_sol[k]\n                    q_gen += self.qg_sol[k]\n\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n            for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5:\n                    continue\n\n                from_bus = int(self.br_from[br_idx]) - 1\n                to_bus = int(self.br_to[br_idx]) - 1\n\n                if from_bus == i:\n                    p_flow += pij[br_idx]\n                    q_flow += qij[br_idx]\n                elif            q_balance = q_gen - self.q_demand[i] + self.bs_shunt[i] * self.vm_sol[i]**2 - q_flow\n\n            max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, abs(p_balance) * self.base_mva)\n            max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, abs(q_balance) * self.base_mva)\n\n        max_voltage_violation = 0\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            if self.vm_sol[i] < self.vmin[i]:\n                max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, self.vmin[i] - self.vm_sol[i])\n            elif self.vm_sol[i] > self.vmax[i]:\n                max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, self.vm_sol[i] - self.vmax[i])\n\n        max_branch_overload = 0\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] > 0.5 and Load network data\n        with open(network_file) as f:\n            self.data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = float(self.data.get('baseMVA', base_mva))\n\n        # Parse network components\n        self.buses = np.array(self.data['bus'], dtype=float)\n        self.generators = np.array(self.data['gen'], dtype=float)\n        self.branches = np.array(self.data['branch'], dtype=float)\n        self.gencost = np.array(self.data.get('gencost', []), dtype=float)\n\n        self.n_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.n_gen = len(self.generators)\n        self.n_branch = len(self.branches)\n\n        # Build bus index mapping\n        self.bus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(self.buses)\n\n        # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format columns)\n        # [BUS_I, TYPE, PD, QD, GS, BS, BUS_AREA, VM, VA, BASE_KV, ZONE, VMAX, VMIN]\n        self.bus_ids = self.buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.bus_type = self.buses[:, 1].astype(int)\n        self.p_demand = self.buses[:, 2] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.q_demand = self.buses[:, 3] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.gs_shunt = self.buses[:, 4] / self.base_mva\n        self.bs_shunt = self.buses[:, 5] / self.base_mva\n        self.vm_init = self.buses[:, 7]\n        self.va_init = np.deg2rad(self.buses[:, 8])\n        self.vmax = self.buses[:, 11]\n        self.vmin = self.buses[:, 12]\n\n        # Extract generator data (MATPOWER format columns)\n        # [GEN_BUS, PG, QG, QMAX, QMIN, VG, MBASE, GEN_STATUS, PMAX, PMIN, ...]\n        self.gen_bus = self.generators[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.pg_init = self.generators[:, 1] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.qg_init = self.generators[:, 2] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.qg_max = self.generators[:, 3] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.qg_min = self.generators[:, 4] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.vg_setpoint = self.generators[:, 5]\n        self.pg_max = self.generators[:, 8] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.pg_min = self.generators[:, 9] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.gen_status = self.generators[:, 7] if len(self.generators[0]) > 7 else np.ones(self.n_gen)\n\n        # Extract branch data (MATPOWER format columns)\n        # [F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, TAP, SHIFT, BR_STATUS, ANGMIN, ANGMAX]\n        self.br_from = self.branches[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.br_to = self.branches[:, 1].astype(int)\n        self.br_r = self.branches[:, 2]\n        self.br_x = self.branches[:, 3]\n        self.br_b = self.branches[:, 4]\n        self.rate_a = self.branches[:, 5] / self.base_mva  # pu\n        self.tap = self.branches[:, 8]\n        self.shift = np.deg2rad(self.branches[:, 9])\n        self.br_status = self.branches[:, 10]\n        self.angmin = np.deg2rad(self.branches[:, 11])\n        self.angmax = np.deg2rad(self.branches[:, 12])\n\n        # Compute series admittances\n        self._compute_series_admittances()\n\n        # Solution storage\n        self.solution = None\n        self.solver_status = None\n\n    def _compute_series_admittances(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute series admittances for all branches\"\"\"\n        self.br_g = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        self.br_b = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for i in range(self.n_branch):\n            r = self.br_r[i]\n            x = self.br_x[i]\n\n            if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x) < 1e-12:\n                self.br_g[i] = 0.0\n                self.br_b[i] = 0.0\n            else:\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                self.br_g[i] = r / z_sq\n                self.br_b[i] = -x / z_sq\n\n    def _compute_branch_flows(self, vm: np.ndarray, va: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute apparent power flows for all branches in MW and MVAr\n        Returns: (pij_mw, qij_mvar, pji_mw, qji_mvar, sij_mva, sji_mva)\n        \"\"\"\n        pij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        qij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        pji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        qji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        sij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        sji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for k in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[k] < 0.5:\n                continue\n\n            # Get bus indices\n            i = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[k])]\n            j = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[k])]\n\n            # Branch parameters\n            g = self.br_g[k]\n            b = self.br_b[k]\n            bc = self.br_b[k]  # charging susceptance (total)\n            tap = self.tap[k] if abs(self.tap[k]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = self.shift[k]\n\n            # Voltage magnitudes and angles\n            vm_i = vm[i]\n            vm_j = vm[j]\n            va_i = va[i]\n            va_j = va[j]\n\n            # Angle differences\n            delta_ij = va_i - va_j - shift\n            delta_ji = va_j - va_i + shift\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            # Power flows (from AC branch pi-model)\n            cos_dij = np.cos(delta_ij)\n            sin_dij = np.sin(delta_ij)\n            cos_dji = np.cos(delta_ji)\n            sin_dji = np.sin(delta_ji)\n\n            # i -> j\n            pij[k] = g * vm_i**2 * inv_t2 - vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * cos_dij + b * sin_dij)\n            qij[k] = -(b + bc/2) * vm_i**2 * inv_t2 - vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * sin_dij - b * cos_dij)\n\n            # j -> i\n            pji[k] = g * vm_j**2 - vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * cos_dji + b * sin_dji)\n            qji[k] = -(b + bc/2) * vm_j**2 - vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * sin_dji - b * cos_dji)\n\n            # Apparent power\n            sij[k] = np.sqrt(pij[k]**2 + qij[k]**2) * self.base_mva\n            sji[k] = np.sqrt(pji[k]**2 + qji[k]**2) * self.base_mva\n\n        return pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji\n\n    def solve(self, verbose: bool = False) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Solve the AC-OPF problem\n        Returns True if optimal solution found\n        \"\"\"\n        # Decision variables\n        vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', self.n_buses)  # voltage magnitudes\n        va = ca.SX.sym('va', self.n_buses)  # voltage angles\n        pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', self.n_gen)    # generator real power\n        qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', self.n_gen)    # generator reactive power\n\n        # Concatenate all variables\n        variables = ca.vertcat(vm, va, pg, qg)\n\n        # Build cost function\n        cost = 0\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if len(self.gencost) > k:\n                # gencost format: [TYPE, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, N, COEFF_1, COEFF_2, ...]\n                coeff = self.gencost[k]\n                if coeff[0] == 2:  # polynomial cost\n                    n = int(coeff[3])\n                    # Quadratic cost: c2*pg^2 + c1*pg + c0\n                    if n >= 1:\n                        c0 = coeff[4 + n - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 1 else 0\n                        c1 = coeff[4 + n - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 2 else 0\n                        c2 = coeff[4 + n - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 3 else 0\n                        cost += c2 * pg[k]**2 + c1 * pg[k] + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        constraints = []\n        constraint_lbs = []\n        constraint_ubs = []\n\n        # 1. Voltage angle at reference buses (slack bus at bus 0)\n        constraints.append(va[0])  # va[0] = 0\n        constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n        constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n        # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            constraints.append(vm[i])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.vmin[i])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.vmax[i])\n\n        # 3. Generator real power bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(pg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.pg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.pg_max[k])\n\n        # 4. Generator reactive power bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(qg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.qg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.qg_max[k])\n\n        # 5. Power flow balance equations\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            # Real power balance\n            p_gen = 0\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i:  # account for 1-based bus indexing\n                    p_gen = p_gen + pg[k]\n\n            # Compute power flows at this bus (sum of flows to adjacent buses)\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5:\n                    continue\n\n                from_bus = int(self.br_from[br_idx]) - 1\n                to_bus = int(self.br_to[br_idx]) - 1\n\n                g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n                b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n                shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n                inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n                inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n                if from_bus == i:\n                    # Power leaving bus i to bus j\n                    delta_ij = va[i] - va[to_bus] - shift\n                    cos_dij = ca.cos(delta_ij)\n                    sin_dij = ca.sin(delta_ij)\n\n                    p_flow = p_flow + (g * vm[i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                                      vm[i] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * cos_dij + b * sin_dij))\n                    q_flow = q_flow + (-(b + bc/2) * vm[i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                                      vm[i] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * sin_dij - b * cos_dij))\n\n                elif to_bus == i:\n                    # Power leaving bus i to bus from_bus\n                    delta_ji = va[i] - va[from_bus] + shift\n                    cos_dji = ca.cos(delta_ji)\n                    sin_dji = ca.sin(delta_ji)\n\n                    p_flow = p_flow + (g * vm[i]**2 -\n                                      vm[i] * vm[from_bus] * inv_t * (g * cos_dji + b * sin_dji))\n                    q_flow = q_flow + (-(b + bc/2) * vm[i]**2 -\n                                      vm[i] * vm[from_bus] * inv_t * (g * sin_dji - b * cos_dji))\n\n            # Real power balance: Pg - Pd - Gs*Vm^2 = P_flow\n            p_balance = p_gen - self.p_demand[i] - self.gs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - p_flow\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n            # Reactive power balance: Qg - Qd + Bs*Vm^2 = Q_flow\n            q_balance = 0\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i:\n                    q_balance = q_balance + qg[k]\n\n            q_balance = q_balance - self.q_demand[i] + self.bs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - q_flow\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n        # 6. Branch thermal limits (apparent power)\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5 or self.rate_a[br_idx] < 1e-6:\n                continue\n\n            from_bus = int(self.br_from[br_idx]) - 1\n            to_bus = int(self.br_to[br_idx]) - 1\n\n            g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n            b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            # i -> j\n            delta_ij = va[from_bus] - va[to_bus] - shift\n            cos_dij = ca.cos(delta_ij)\n            sin_dij = ca.sin(delta_ij)\n\n            p_ij = (g * vm[from_bus]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                   vm[from_bus] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * cos_dij + b * sin_dij))\n            q_ij = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[from_bus]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                   vm[from_bus] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * sin_dij - b * cos_dij))\n            s_ij = ca.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n\n            constraints.append(s_ij)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx])\n\n            # j -> i\n            delta_ji = va[to_bus] - va[from_bus] + shift\n            cos_dji = ca.cos(delta_ji)\n            sin_dji = ca.sin(delta_ji)\n\n            p_ji = (g * vm[to_bus]**2 -\n                   vm[from_bus] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * cos_dji + b * sin_dji))\n            q_ji = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[to_bus]**2 -\n                   vm[from_bus] * vm[to_bus] * inv_t * (g * sin_dji - b * cos_dji))\n            s_ji = ca.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            constraints.append(s_ji)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx])\n\n        # Build NLP\n        nlp = {\n            'x': variables,\n            'f': cost,\n            'g': ca.vertcat(*constraints) if constraints else ca.DM([])\n        }\n\n        # Create solver\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 5 if verbose else 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 1000,\n            'print_time': verbose,\n        }\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.concatenate([\n            self.vm_init,\n            self.va_init,\n            self.pg_init,\n            self.qg_init\n        ])\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = np.concatenate([\n            self.vmin,\n            -np.pi * np.ones(self.n_buses),\n            self.pg_min,\n            self.qg_min\n        ])\n\n        ubx = np.concatenate([\n            self.vmax,\n            np.pi * np.ones(self.n_buses),\n            self.pg_max,\n            self.qg_max\n        ])\n\n        # Solve\n        result = solver(\n            x0=x0,\n            lbx=lbx,\n            ubx=ubx,\n            lbg=np.array(constraint_lbs),\n            ubg=np.array(constraint_ubs)\n        )\n\n        # Extract solution\n        sol = result['x'].full().flatten()\n        self.vm_sol = sol[:self.n_buses]\n        self.va_sol = sol[self.n_buses:2*self.n_buses]\n        self.pg_sol = sol[2*self.n_buses:2*self.n_buses + self.n_gen]\n        self.qg_sol = sol[2*self.n_buses + self.n_gen:]\n\n        # Check solver status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        self.solver_status = stats['return_status']\n\n        if verbose:\n            print(f\"Solver status: {self.solver_status}\")\n\n        return self.solver_status == 'Solve_Succeeded'\n\n    def generate_report(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n        if self.vm_sol is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Solver has not been run successfully\")\n\n        # Compute final branch flows\n        pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji = self._compute_branch_flows(self.vm_sol, self.va_sol)\n\n        # Convert to MW and MVAr\n        pg_mw = self.pg_sol * self.base_mva\n        qg_mvar = self.qg_sol * self.base_mva\n        sij_mva = sij\n        sji_mva = sji\n\n        # Calculate totals\n        total_gen_mw = np.sum(pg_mw)\n        total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_mvar)\n        total_load_mw = np.sum(self.p_demand) * self.base_mva\n        total_load_mvar = np.sum(self.q_demand) * self.base_mva\n\n        # Calculate losses\n        total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n        # Calculate cost\n        total_cost = 0\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if len(self.gencost) > k:\n                coeff = self.gencost[k]\n                if coeff[0] == 2:\n                    n = int(coeff[3])\n                    if n >= 1:\n                        c0 = coeff[4 + n - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 1 else 0\n                        c1 = coeff[4 + n - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 2 else 0\n                        c2 = coeff[4 + n - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 3 else 0\n                        total_cost += c2 * self.pg_sol[k]**2 + c1 * self.pg_sol[k] + c0\n                        total_cost = float(total_cost) * self.base_mva\n\n        # Build generators section\n        generators = []\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            gen_bus_idx = int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1\n            generators.append({\n                \"id\": k + 1,\n                \"bus\": int(self.gen_bus[k]),\n                \"pg_MW\": float(pg_mw[k]),\n                \"qg_MVAr\": float(qg_mvar[k]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(self.pg_min[k] * self.base_mva),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(self.pg_max[k] * self.base_mva),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(self.qg_min[k] * self.base_mva),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(self.qg_max[k] * self.base_mva)\n            })\n\n        # Build buses section\n        buses = []\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            buses.append({\n                \"id\": int(self.bus_ids[i]),\n                \"vm_pu\": float(self.vm_sol[i]),\n                \"va_deg\": float(np.rad2deg(self.va_sol[i])),\n                \"vmin_pu\": float(self.vmin[i]),\n                \"vmax_pu\": float(self.vmax[i])\n            })\n\n        # Find most loaded branches\n        branch_loadings = []\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5 or self.rate_a[br_idx] < 1e-6:\n                continue\n\n            max_flow = max(sij_mva[br_idx], sji_mva[br_idx])\n            loading_pct = 100.0 * max_flow / (self.rate_a[br_idx] * self.base_mva)\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                \"from_bus\": int(self.br_from[br_idx]),\n                \"to_bus\": self.branches[:, 8]\n        self.shift = np.deg2rad(self.branches[:, 9])\n        self.br_status = self.branches[:, 10]\n        self.angmin = np.deg2rad(self.branches[:, 11])\n        self.angmax = np.deg2rad(self.branches[:, 12])\n\n        # Compute series admittances\n        self._compute_series_admittances()\n\n        # Solution storage\n        self.vm_sol = None\n        self.va_sol = None\n        self.pg_sol = None\n        self.qg_sol = None\n        self.solver_status = None\n\n    def _compute_series_admittances(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute series admittances for all branches\"\"\"\n        self.br_g = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        self.br_b = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for i in range(self.n_branch):\n            r = self.br_r[i]\n            x = self.br_x[i]\n\n            if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x) < 1e-12:\n                self.br_g[i] = 0.0\n                self.br_b[i] = 0.0\n            else:\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                self.br_g[i] = r / z_sq\n                self.br_b[i] = -x / z_sq\n\n    def _build_ybus(self):\n        \"\"\"Build admittance matrix\"\"\"\n        ybus = np.zeros((self.n_buses, self.n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n        for k in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[k] < 0.5:\n                continue\n\n            from_bus = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[k])]\n            to_bus = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[k])]\n\n            g = self.br_g[k]\n            b = self.br_b[k]\n            bc = self.br_b[k] / 2.0\n            tap = self.tap[k] if abs(self.tap[k]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = self.shift[k]\n\n            # Series element\n            y_series = g + 1j * b\n\n            # Shunt elements\n            y_shunt = 1j * bc\n\n            # Tap complex number\n            tap_complex = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap_complex\n            inv_t2_mag = abs(inv_t)**2\n\n            # Add to diagonal (from side)\n            ybus[from_bus, from_bus] += (y_series + y_shunt) * inv_t2_mag\n\n            # Add to diagonal (to side)\n            ybus[to_bus, to_bus] += y_series + y_shunt\n\n            # Add to off-diagonal\n            ybus[from_bus, to_bus] -= y_series * inv_t\n            ybus[to_bus, from_bus] -= y_series * np.conj(inv_t)\n\n        return ybus\n\n    def _dc_opf_init(self):\n        \"\"\"Run DC-OPF for initial point\"\"\"\n        # Build DC power flow model using admittances\n        ybus = self._build_ybus()\n        b_matrix = -np.imag(ybus)\n\n        # Remove slack bus row and column for reduced system\n        slack_bus = 0\n        indices = list(range(self.n_buses))\n        indices.remove(slack_bus)\n\n        B_reduced = b_matrix[np.ix_(indices, indices)]\n        B_slack = b_matrix[np.ix_(indices, [slack_bus])]\n\n        # Net load (demand - generation) in pu\n        p_net = self.p_demand.copy()\n\n        # Add shunt power\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            p_net[i] += self.gs_shunt[i] * self.vm_init[i]**2\n\n        p_net_reduced = p_net[indices]\n\n        try:\n            # Solve reduced system\n            B_inv = np.linalg.pinv(B_reduced)\n            va_reduced = -B_inv @ p_net_reduced\n\n            # Reconstruction\n            self.va_init[slack_bus] = 0\n            for i, idx in enumerate(indices):\n                self.va_init[idx] = va_reduced[i]\n        except:\n            pass  # Keep initial angles\n\n        # Distribute generation\n        total_demand = np.sum(self.p_demand)\n        active_gens = np.where(self.gen_status > 0.5)[0]\n\n        if len(active_gens) > 0:\n            # Simple dispatch: pro-rata among generators\n            dispatch = total_demand / len(active_gens)\n            for k in active_gens:\n                pg_new = max(self.pg_min[k], min(self.pg_max[k], dispatch))\n                self.pg_init[k] = pg_new\n\n    def solve_ac(self, verbose: bool = False) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n        # Get initial point from DC-OPF\n        self._dc_opf_init()\n\n        # Decision variables\n        pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', self.n_gen)\n        qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', self.n_gen)\n        vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', self.n_buses)\n        va = ca.SX.sym('va', self.n_buses)\n\n        variables = ca.vertcat(pg, qg, vm, va)\n        n_vars = 2 * self.n_gen + 2 * self.n_buses\n\n        # Build cost function (sum of generator costs)\n        cost = 0\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*pg^2 + c1*pg + c0 in $/MWh\n            if len(self.gencost) > k:\n                coeff = self.gencost[k]\n                if len(coeff) >= 5 and coeff[0] == 2:\n                    n_coeff = int(coeff[3])\n                    if n_coeff >= 2:\n                        # Coefficients are in order: highest degree first\n                        c0 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 1 else 0\n                        c1 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 2 else 0\n                        c2 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 3 else 0\n                        # Cost is c2*pg^2 + c1*pg + c0, where pg is in MW already\n                        # Divide by baseMVA since pg variables are in pu\n                        cost += (c2 * (pg[k] * self.base_mva)**2 +\n                                c1 * (pg[k] * self.base_mva) + c0)\n\n        # Constraints\n        constraints = []\n        constraint_lbs = []\n        constraint_ubs = []\n\n        # Variable bounds constraints (explicit for tighter control)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(pg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.pg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.pg_max[k])\n\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(qg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.qg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.qg_max[k])\n\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            constraints.append(vm[i])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.vmin[i])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.vmax[i])\n\n        # Angle constraints\n        constraints.append(va[0])  # Slack bus\n        constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n        constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n        # Power flow balance equations\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            # Generator power at bus i\n            p_gen = 0\n            q_gen = 0\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i:\n                    p_gen += pg[k]\n                    q_gen += qg[k]\n\n            # Branch power flows from/to bus i\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5:\n                    continue\n\n                from_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])]\n                to_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])]\n\n                g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n                b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n                shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n                inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n                inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n                if from_idx == i:\n                    delta = va[from_idx] - va[to_idx] - shift\n                    p_ij = (g * vm[from_idx]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                           vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                           (g * ca.cos(delta) + b * ca.sin(delta)))\n                    q_ij = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[from_idx]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                           vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                           (g * ca.sin(delta) - b * ca.cos(delta)))\n                    p_flow += p_ij\n                    q_flow += q_ij\n\n                elif to_idx == i:\n                    delta = va[to_idx] - va[from_idx] + shift\n                    p_ji = (g * vm[to_idx]**2 -\n                           vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                           (g * ca.cos(delta) + b * ca.sin(delta)))\n                    q_ji = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[to_idx]**2 -\n                           vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                           (g * ca.sin(delta) - b * ca.cos(delta)))\n                    p_flow += p_ji\n                    q_flow += q_ji\n\n            # Power balance\n            p_balance = (p_gen - self.p_demand[i] - self.gs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - p_flow)\n            q_balance = (q_gen - self.q_demand[i] + self.bs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - q_flow)\n\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(-1e-3)\n            constraint_ubs.append(1e-3)\n\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(-1e-3)\n            constraint_ubs.append(1e-3)\n\n        # Branch MVA limits\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5 or self.rate_a[br_idx] < 1e-6:\n                continue\n\n            from_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])]\n            to_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])]\n\n            g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n            b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            # i -> j\n            delta_ij = va[from_idx] - va[to_idx] - shift\n            p_ij = (g * vm[from_idx]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                   vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                   (g * ca.cos(delta_ij) + b * ca.sin(delta_ij)))\n            q_ij = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[from_idx]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                   vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                   (g * ca.sin(delta_ij) - b * ca.cos(delta_ij)))\n            s_ij = ca.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n\n            constraints.append(s_ij)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx] * 1.2)  # Allow some slack\n\n            # j -> i\n            delta_ji = va[to_idx] - va[from_idx] + shift\n            p_ji = (g * vm[to_idx]**2 -\n                   vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                   (g * ca.cos(delta_ji) + b * ca.sin(delta_ji)))\n            q_ji = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[to_idx]**2 -\n                   vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                   (g * ca.sin(delta_ji) - b * ca.cos(delta_ji)))\n            s_ji = ca.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            constraints.append(s_ji)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx] * 1.2)\n\n        # Build NLP\n        nlp = {\n            'x': variables,\n            'f': cost,\n            'g': ca.vertcat(*constraints)\n        }\n\n        # Solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 500,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n            'print_time': False,\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.concatenate([\n            self.pg_init,\n            self.qg_init,\n            self.vm_init,\n            self.va_init\n        ])\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = np.concatenate([\n            self.pg_min, self.qg_min,\n            self.vmin,\n            -np.pi * np.ones(self.n_buses)\n        ])\n\n        ubx = np.concatenate([\n            self.pg_max, self.qg_max,\n            self.vmax,\n            np.pi * np.ones(self.n_buses)\n        ])\n\n        # Solve\n        result = solver(\n            x0=x0,\n            lbx=lbx,\n            ubx=ubx,\n            lbg=np.array(constraint_lbs),\n            ubg=np.array(constraint_ubs)\n        )\n\n        # Extract solution\n        sol = result['x'].full().flatten()\n        self.pg_sol = sol[:self.n_gen]\n        self.qg_sol = sol[self.n_gen:2*self.n_gen]\n        self.vm_sol = sol[2*self.n_gen:2*self.n_gen + self.n_buses]\n        self.va_sol = sol[2*self.n_gen + self.n_buses:]\n\n        # Check status\n        stats = solver.stats()\n        self.solver_status = stats['return_status']\n\n        if verbose:\n            print(f\"Solver status: {self.solver_status}\")\n\n        return self.solver_status == 'Solve_Succeeded'\n\n    def _compute_branch_flows_final(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute final branch flows\"\"\"\n        pij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        qij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        pji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        qji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        sij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        sji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5:\n                continue\n\n            from_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])]\n            to_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])]\n\n            g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n            b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n            vm_i = self.vm_sol[from_idx]\n            vm_j = self.vm_sol[to_idx]\n            va_i = self.va_sol[from_idx]\n            va_j = self.va_sol[to_idx]\n\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            # i -> j\n            delta_ij = va_i - va_j - shift\n            cos_ij = np.cos(delta_ij)\n            sin_ij = np.sin(delta_ij)\n\n            pij[br_idx] = (g * vm_i**2 * inv_t2 -\n                          vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * cos_ij + b * sin_ij))\n            qij[br_idx] = (-(b + bc/2) * vm_i**2 * inv_t2 -\n                          vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * sin_ij - b * cos_ij))\n            sij[br_idx] = np.sqrt(pij[br_idx]**2 + qij[br_idx]**2) * self.base_mva\n\n            # j -> i\n            delta_ji = va_j - va_i + shift\n            cos_ji = np.cos(delta_ji)\n            sin_ji = np.sin(delta_ji)\n\n            pji[br_idx] = (g * vm_j**2 -\n                          vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * cos_ji + b * sin_ji))\n            qji[br_idx] = (-(b + bc/2) * vm_j**2 -\n                          vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * sin_ji - b * cos_ji))\n            sji[br_idx] = np.sqrt(pji[br_idx]**2 + qji[br_idx]**2) * self.base_mva\n\n        return pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji\n\n    def generate_report(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n        if self.pg_sol is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Solver has not been run successfully\")\n\n        # Compute flows\n        pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji = self._compute_branch_flows_final()\n\n        # Convert to MW/MVAr\n        pg_mw = self.pg_sol * self.base_mva\n        qg_mvar = self.qg_sol * self.base_mva\n\n        # Calculate totals\n        total_gen_mw = np.sum(pg_mw)\n        total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_mvar)\n        total_load_mw = np.sum(self.p_demand) * self.base_mva\n        total_load_mvar = np.sum(self.q_demand) * self.base_mva\n        total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n        # Calculate cost\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if len(self.gencost) > k:\n                coeff = self.gencost[k]\n                if len(coeff) >= 5 and coeff[0] == 2:\n                    n_coeff = int(coeff[3])\n                    if n_coeff >= 2:\n                        c0 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 1 else 0\n                        c1 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 2 else 0\n                        c2 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 3 else 0\n                        total_cost += c2 * (pg_mw[k])**2 + c1 * pg_mw[k] + c0\n\n        # Generators\n        generators = []\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            generators.append({\n                \"id\": k + 1,\n                \"bus\": int(self.gen_bus[k]),\n                \"pg_MW\": float(pg_mw[k]),\n                \"qg_MVAr\": float(qg_mvar[k]),\n                \"pmin_MW\": float(self.pg_min[k] * self.base_mva),\n                \"pmax_MW\": float(self.pg_max[k] * self.base_mva),\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": float(self.qg_min[k] * self.base_mva),\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": float(self.qg_max[k] * self.base_mva)\n            })\n\n        # Buses\n        buses = []\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            buses.append({\n                \"id\": int(self.bus_ids[i]),\n                \"vm_pu\": float(self.vm_sol[i]),\n                \"va_deg\": float(np.rad2deg(self.va_sol[i])),\n                \"vmin_pu\": float(self.vmin[i]),\n                \"vmax_pu\": float(self.vmax[i])\n            })\n\n        # Most loaded branches\n        branch_loadings = []\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5 or self.rate_a[br_idx] < 1e-6:\n                continue\n\n            max_flow = max(sij[br_idx], sji[br_idx])\n            loading_pct = 100.0 * max_flow / (self.rate_a[br_idx] * self.base_mva)\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                \"from_bus\": int(self.br_from[br_idx]),\n                \"to_bus\": int(self.br_to[br_idx]),\n                \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n                \"flow_from_MVA\": float(sij[br_idx]),\n                \"flow_to_MVA\": float(sji[br_idx]),\n                \"limit_MVA\": float(self.rate_a[br_idx] * self.base_mva)\n            })\n\n        branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n        most_loaded = branch_loadings[:10]\n\n        # Feasibility\n        max_p_mismatch = 0\n        max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            p_gen = sum(self.pg_sol[k] for k in range(self.n_gen)\n                       if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i)\n            q_gen = sum(self.qg_sol[k] for k in range(self.n_gen)\n                       if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i)\n\n            p_flow = sum(pij[br_idx] for br_idx in range(self.n_branch)\n                        if self.br_status[br_idx] > 0.5 and\n                        self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])] == i)\n            p_flow += sum(pji[br_idx] for br_idx in range(self.n_branch)\n                         if self.br_status[br_idx] > 0.5 and\n                         self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])] == i)\n\n            q_flow = sum(qij[br_idx] for br_idx in range(self.n_branch)\n                        if self.br_status[br_idx] > 0.5 and\n                        self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])] == i)\n            q_flow += sum(qji[br_idx] for br_idx in range(self.n_branch)\n                         if self.br_status[br_idx] > 0.5 and\n                         self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])] == i)\n\n            p_mismatch = abs((p_gen - self.p_demand[i] -\n                             self.gs_shunt[i] * self.vm_sol[i]**2 - p_flow) *\n                            self.base_mva)\n            q_mismatch = abs((q_gen - self.q_demand[i] +\n                             self.bs_shunt[i] * self.vm_sol[i]**2 - q_flow) *\n                            self.base_mva)\n\n            max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n            max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n        max_v_violation = max(\n            max(0, self.vmin[i] - self.vm_sol[i])\n            for i in range(self.n_buses)\n        )\n        max_v_violation = max(\n            max_v_violation,\n            max(0, self.vm_sol[i] - self.vmax[i] for i in range(self.n_buses))\n        )\n\n        max_branch_overload = 0\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] > 0.5 and self.rate_a[br_idx] > 1e-6:\n                max_flow = max(sij[br_idx], sji[br_idx])\n                limit = self.rate_a[br_idx] * self.base_mva\n                if max_flow > limit:\n                    max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, max_flow - limit)\n\n        report = {\n            \"summary\": {\n                \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(total_cost),\n                \"total_load_MW\": float(total_load_mw),\n                \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_load_mvar),\n                \"total_generation_MW\": float(total_gen_mw),\n                \"total_generation_MVAr\": float(total_gen_mvar),\n                \"total_losses_MW\": float(total_losses_mw),\n                \"solver_status\": \"optimal\" if self.solver_status == 'Solve_Succeeded' else \"suboptimal\"\n            },\n            \"generators\": generators,\n            \"buses\": buses,\n            \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n            \"feasibility_check\": {\n                \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": float(max_p_mismatch),\n                \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": float(max_q_mismatch),\n                \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": float(max_v_violation),\n                \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": float(max_branch_overload)\n            }\n        }\n\n        return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Run solver and generate branches\"\"\"\n        self.br_g = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        self.br_b = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for i in range(self.n_branch):\n            r = self.br_r[i]\n            x =        self.br_g[i] = r / z_sq\n                self.br_b[i] = -x / z_sq\n\n    def _build_ybus(self):\n        \"\"\"Build admittance matrix\"\"\"\n        ybus = np.zeros((self.n_buses, self.n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n        for k in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[k] < 0.5:\n                continue\n\n            from_bus = 2.0\n            tap = self.tap[k] if abs(self.tap[k]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = self.shift[k]\n\n            # Series element\n            y_series = g + 1j * b\n\n            # Shunt elements\n            y_shunt = 1j * bc\n\n            # Tap complex number\n            tap_complex = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap_complex\n            inv_t2_mag = abs(inv_t)**2\n\n            # Add to diagonal (from side)\n            ybus[from_bus, from_bus] += (y_series + y_shunt) * inv_t2_mag\n\n            # Add to diagonal (to side)\n            ybus[to_bus, to_bus] += y_series + y_shunt\n\n            # Add to off-diagonal\n            ybus[from_bus, to_bus] -= y_series * inv_t\n            ybus[to_bus, from_bus] -= y_series * np.conj(inv_t)\n\n        return ybus\n\n    def _dc_opf_init(self):\n        \"\"\"Run DC-OPF for initial point\"\"\"\n        # Build DC power flow model using admittances\n        ybus = self._build_ybus()\n        b_matrix = -np.imag(ybus)\n\n        # Remove slack bus row and column for reduced system\n        slack_bus = 0\n        indices = list(range(self.n_buses))\n        indices.remove(slack_bus)\n\n        B_reduced = b_matrix[np.ix_(indices, indices)]\n        B_slack = b_matrix[np.ix_(indices, [slack_bus])]\n\n        # Net load (demand - generation) in pu\n        p_net = self.p_demand.copy()\n\n        # Add shunt power\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            p_net[i] += self.gs_shunt[i] * self.vm_init[i]**2\n\n        p_net_reduced =        self.va_init[slack_bus] = 0\n            for i, idx in enumerate(indices):\n                self.va_init[idx] = va_reduced[i]\n        except:\n            pass  # Keep initial angles\n\n pro-rata among generators\n            dispatch = total_demand / len(active_gens)\n            for k in active_gens:\n                pg_new = max(self.pg_min[k], min(self.pg_max[k], dispatch))\n                self.pg_init[k] = pg_new\n\n    def solve_ac(self, verbose: bool = False) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n        # Get initial point from DC-OPF\n        self._dc_opf_init()\n\n        # Decision variables\n        pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', self.n_gen)\n        qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', self.n_gen)\n        vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', self.n_buses)\n        va = ca.SX.sym('va', self.n_buses)\n\n        variables = ca.vertcat(pg, qg, vm, va)\n        n_vars = 2 * self.n_gen + 2 * self.n_buses\n\n        # Build cost function (sum of generator costs)\n        cost = 0\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            # Quadratic cost: c2*pg^2 + c1*pg + c0 in $/MWh\n            if len(self.gencost) > k:\n                coeff = self.gencost[k]\n                if len(coeff) >= 5 and coeff[0] == 2:\n                    n_coeff = int(coeff[3])\n                    if n_coeff >= 2:\n                        # Coefficients are in order: highest degree first\n                        c0 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 1 else 0\n                        c1 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 2 else 0\n                        c2 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 3 else 0\n                        # Cost is c2*pg^2 + c1*pg + c0, where pg is in MW already\n                        # Divide by baseMVA since pg variables are in pu\n                        cost += (c2 * (pg[k] * self.base_mva)**2 +\n                                c1 * (pg[k] * self.base_mva) + c0)\n\n        # Constraints\n        constraints = []\n        constraint_lbs = []\n        constraint_ubs = []\n\n        # Variable bounds constraints (explicit for tighter control)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(pg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.pg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.pg_max[k])\n\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(qg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.qg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.qg_max[k])\n\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            constraints.append(vm[i])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.vmin[i])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.vmax[i])\n\n        # Angle constraints\n        constraints.append(va[0])  # Slack bus\n        constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n        constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n        # Power flow balance equations\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            # Generator power at bus i\n            p_gen = 0\n            q_gen = 0\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i:\n                    p_gen += pg[k]\n                    q_gen += qg[k]\n\n            # Branch power flows from/to bus i\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5:\n                    continue\n\n                from_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])]\n                to_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])]\n\n                g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n                b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n                shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n                inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n                inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n                if from_idx == i:\n                    delta = va[from_idx] - va[to_idx] -                    q_ij = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[from_idx]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                           vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                           (g * ca.sin(delta) - b *         p_ji = (g * vm[to_idx]**2 -\n                           vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n                           (g * ca.cos(delta) + b * ca.sin(delta)))\n                    q_ji = p_ji\n                    q_flow += q_ji\n\n            # Power balance\n            p_balance = (p_gen - self.p_demand[i] - self.gs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - p_flow)\n            q_balance = (q_gen - self.q_demand[i] + self.bs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - q_flow)\n\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(-1e-3)\n            constraint_ubs.append(1e-3)\n\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(-1e-3)\n            constraint_ubs.append(1e-3)\n\n        # Branch MVA limits\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5 or self.rate_a[br_idx] < 1e-6:\n                continue\n\n            from_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])]\n            to_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])]\n\n            g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n            b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            tap = self.tap[br_idx] if inv_t *\n                   (g * ca.cos(delta_ji) + b * ca.sin(delta_ji)))\n            q_ji = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[to_idx]**2 -\n                   vm[from_idx] * vm[to_idx] * inv_t *\n constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx] * 1.2)\n\n        # Build NLP\n        nlp = {\n            'x': variables,\n            'f': cost,\n            'g': ca.vertcat(*constraints)\n        }\n\n        # Solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 500,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n            'print_time': False,\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.concatenate([\n            self.pg_init,\n            self.qg_init,\n _compute_branch_flows_final(self):\n        \"\"\"Compute final branch flows\"\"\"\n        pij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        qij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        pji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        qji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        sij = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n        sji = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5:\n                continue\n\n            from_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])]\n            to_idx = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])]\n\n            g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n            b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n            vm_i = self.vm_sol[from_idx]\n            vm_j = self.vm_sol[to_idx]\n            va_i = self.va_sol[from_idx]\n            va_j = self.va_sol[to_idx]\n\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            # i -> j\n            delta_ij = va_i - va_j - shift\n            cos_ij = np.cos(delta_ij)\n            sin_ij = np.sin(delta_ij)\n\n            pij[br_idx] = (g * vm_i**2 * inv_t2 -\n                          vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * cos_ij + b * sin_ij))\n            qij[br_idx] = (-(b + bc/2) * vm_i**2 * inv_t2 -\n                          vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * sin_ij - b * cos_ij))\n            sij[br_idx] = np.sqrt(pij[br_idx]**2 + qij[br_idx]**2) * self.base_mva\n\n            # j -> i\n            delta_ji = va_j - va_i + shift\n            cos_ji = np.cos(delta_ji)\n            sin_ji = np.sin(delta_ji)\n\n            pji[br_idx] = (g * vm_j**2 -\n                          vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * cos_ji + b * sin_ji))\n            qji[br_idx] = (-(b + bc/2) * vm_j**2 -\n                          vm_i * vm_j * inv_t * (g * sin_ji - b * cos_ji))\n            sji[br_idx] = np.sqrt(pji[br_idx]**2 + qji[br_idx]**2) * self.base_mva\n\n        return pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji\n\n    def generate_report(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n        if self.pg_sol is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Solver has not been run successfully\")\n\n        # Compute flows\n        pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji = for i in range(self.n_branch):\n            r = self.br_r[i]\n            x = self.br_x[i]\n\n            if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x) < 1e-12:\n                self.br_g[i] = 0.0\n       c2 = coeff[4 + n_coeff - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n_coeff - 3 else 0\n                        # Cost is c2*pg^2 + c1*pg + c0, where pg is in MW already\n                        # Divide by baseMVA constraints = []\n        constraint_lbs = []\n        constraint_ubs = []\n\n        # Variable bounds constraints (explicit for tighter control)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n                b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                bc = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                tap = self.tap[br_idx] if abs(self.tap[br_idx]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n                shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n                inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n                inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n                if from_idx == i:\n                    delta = va[from_idx] - va[to_idx] - Parse data\n        self.buses = np.array(self.data['bus'], dtype=float)\n        self.generators = np.array(self.data['gen'], dtype=float)\n        self.branches = np.array(self.data['branch'], dtype=float)\n        self.gencost = np.array(self.data.get('gencost', []), dtype=float)\n\n        self.n_buses = len(self.buses)\n        self.n_gen = len(self.generators)\n        self.n_branch = len(self.branches)\n\n        self.bus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(self.buses)\n\n        # Variables: [pg_0..pg_{n-1}, qg_0..qg_{n-1}, vm_0..vm_{n-1}, va_0..va_{n-1}]\n        pg = ca.SX.sym('pg', self.n_gen)\n        qg = ca.SX.sym('qg', self.n_gen)\n        vm = ca.SX.sym('vm', self.n_buses)\n        va = ca.SX.sym('va', self.n_buses)\n\n        variables = ca.vertcat(pg, qg, vm, va)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        cost = 0\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if len(self.gencost) > k:\n                coeff = self.gencost[k]\n                if len(coeff) >= 5 and coeff[0] == 2:\n                    n = int(coeff[3])\n                    if n >= 2:\n                        c0 = coeff[4 + n - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 1 else 0\n                        c1 = coeff[4 + n - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 2 else 0\n                        c2 = coeff[4 + n - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 3 else 0\n                        # Cost coefficients already in $/MWh\n                        pg_mw = pg[k] * self.base_mva\n                        cost += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        constraints = []\n        constraint_lbs = []\n        constraint_ubs = []\n\n        # 1. Generator bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(pg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.pg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.pg_max[k])\n\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(qg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.qg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.qg_max[k])\n\n        # 2. Voltage bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            constraints.append(vm[i])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.vmin[i])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.vmax[i])\n\n        # 3. Slack bus angle\n        constraints.append(va[0])\n        constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n        constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n        # 4. Power balance equations\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            # Generator power at bus\n            p_gen = 0\n            q_gen = 0\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - b * cos_d))\n\n                    p_flow = p_flow + p\n                    q_flow = q_flow + q\n\n                elif to_i == i:\n                    # Power from bus j to bus i\n                    delta = va[to_i] - va[from_i] + shift\n                    cos_d = ca.cos(delta)\n                    sin_d = ca.sin(delta)\n\n                    p = (g * vm[to_i]**2 -\n                         vm[from_i] * vm[to_i] * inv_t * (g * cos_d + b * sin_d))\n                    q = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[to_i]**2 -\n                         vm[from_i] * vm[to_i] * inv_t * (g * sin_d - b * cos_d))\n\n                    p_flow = p_flow + p\n                    q_flow = q_flow + q\n\n            # Power balance with relaxed tolerance\n            p_balance = p_gen - self.p_demand[i] - self.gs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - p_flow\n            q_balance = q_gen - self.q_demand[i] + self.bs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - q_flow\n\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(-0.01)  # Relaxed tolerance (0.01 pu = 1 MW in 100 MVA base)\n            constraint_ubs.append(0.01)\n\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(-0.01)\n            constraint_ubs.append(0.01)\n\n        # 5. Branch MVA limits (with relaxation)\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5 or self.rate_a[br_idx] < 0.05:\n                continue\n\n    bc = self.br_b_char[br_idx]\n            t = self.tap[br_idx]\n            shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n            inv_t = 1.0 / t\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            # i -> j\n            delta_ij = va[from_i] - va[to_i] - shift\n            cos_ij = ca.cos(delta_ij)\n            sin_ij = ca.sin(delta_ij)\n\n            p_ij = (g * vm[from_i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n           s_ij = ca.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n\n            constraints.append(s_ij)\n            constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx] * 1.5)  # 50% constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx] * 1.5)\n\n        # Build NLP\n        nlp = {\n            'x': variables,\n            'f': cost,\n            'g': ca.vertcat(*constraints)\n        }\n\n        # Solver\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 1000,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_constr_viol_tol': 1e-2,\n            'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n            'print_time': False,\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial point\n        x0 = np.concatenate([self.pg_init, self.qg_init, self.vm_init, self.va_init])\n\n        # Bounds\n        lbx = np.concatenate([\n            self.pg_min, self.qg_min,\n            self.vmin,\n )\n\n        # Extract solution\n        sol = result['x'].full().flatten()\n        self.pg_sol = sol[:self.n_gen]\n        self.qg_sol = sol[self.n_gen:2*self.n_gen]\n        self.vm_sol = return pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji\n\n    def report(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Generate report\"\"\"\n        pij, qij, pji, qji, sij, sji = self._compute_flows()\n\n        # Convert to self.rate_a[br_idx] < 0.05:\n                continue\n\n            max_flow = max(sij[br_idx], sji[br_idx])\n            loading_pct = 100.0 * max_flow / (self.rate_a[br_idx] * self.base_mva)\n\n
