Objective: minimize generation cost\n        cost = 0\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if len(self.gencost) > k:\n                coeff = self.gencost[k]\n                if len(coeff) >= 5 and coeff[0] == 2:\n                    n = int(coeff[3])\n                    if n >= 2:\n                        c0 = coeff[4 + n - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 1 else 0\n                        c1 = coeff[4 + n - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 2 else 0\n                        c2 = coeff[4 + n - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 3 else 0\n                        # Cost coefficients already in $/MWh\n                        pg_mw = pg[k] * self.base_mva\n                        cost += c2 * pg_mw**2 + c1 * pg_mw + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        constraints = []\n        constraint_lbs = []\n        constraint_ubs = []\n\n        # b * cos_d))\n\n                    p_flow = p_flow + p\n                    q_flow = q_flow + q\n\n            # Power balance with relaxed tolerance\n            p_balance = p_gen - self.p_demand[i] - constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx] * 1.5)\n\n        # Build NLP\n        nlp = {\n            'x': variables,\n            'f': cost,\n            'g': ca.vertcat(*constraints)\n        }\n\n Generator bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(pg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.pg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.pg_max[k])\n\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            constraints.append(qg[k])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.qg_min[k])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.qg_max[k])\n\n        # 2. Voltage bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            constraints.append(vm[i])\n            constraint_lbs.append(self.vmin[i])\n            constraint_ubs.append(self.vmax[i])\n\n        # 3. Slack bus angle\n        constraints.append(va[0])\n        constraint_lbs.append(0.0)\n        constraint_ubs.append(0.0)\n\n        # 4. Power balance equations\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            # Generator power at bus\n            p_gen = 0\n            q_gen = 0\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if int(self.gen_bus[k]) - 1 == i:\n                    p_gen = p_gen + pg[k]\n                    q_gen = q_gen + qg[k]\n\n            # Branch flows\n            p_flow = 0\n            q_flow = 0\n\n            for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5:\n                    continue\n\n                from_i = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])]\n                to_i = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])]\n\n                g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n                b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n                bc = self.br_b_char[br_idx]\n                t = self.tap[br_idx]\n                shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n                inv_t = 1.0 / t\n                inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n                if from_i == i:\n                    # Power from bus i to bus j\n                    delta = va[from_i] - va[to_i] - shift\n                    cos_d = ca.cos(delta)\n                    sin_d = ca.sin(delta)\n\n                    p = (g * vm[from_i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                         vm[from_i] * vm[to_i] * inv_t * (g * cos_d + b * sin_d))\n                    q = (-(b + bc/2) * vm[from_i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                         vm[from_i] * vm[to_i] * inv_t * (g * sin_d - b * cos_d))\n\n                    p_flow = p_flow + p\n                    q_flow = q_flow + q\n\n                elif to_i == i:\n                    # Power from bus j to bus i\n                    delta = va[to_i] - b * cos_d))\n\n                    p_flow = p_flow + p\n                    q_flow = q_flow + q\n\n            # Power balance with relaxed tolerance\n            p_balance = p_gen - self.p_demand[i] - self.gs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - p_flow\n            q_balance = q_gen - self.q_demand[i] + self.bs_shunt[i] * vm[i]**2 - q_flow\n\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(-0.01)  # Relaxed tolerance (0.01 pu = 1 MW in 100 MVA base)\n            constraint_ubs.append(0.01)\n\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n            constraint_lbs.append(-0.01)\n            constraint_ubs.append(0.01)\n\n        # 5. Branch MVA limits (with relaxation)\n        for br_idx in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.br_status[br_idx] < 0.5 or self.rate_a[br_idx] < 0.05:\n                continue\n\n            from_i = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_from[br_idx])]\n            to_i = self.bus_id_to_idx[int(self.br_to[br_idx])]\n\n            g = self.br_g[br_idx]\n            b = self.br_b[br_idx]\n            bc = self.br_b_char[br_idx]\n            t = self.tap[br_idx]\n            shift = self.shift[br_idx]\n\n            inv_t = 1.0 / t\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            # i -> j\n constraint_ubs.append(self.rate_a[br_idx] * 1.5)\n\n        # Build NLP\n        nlp = {\n            'x': variables,\n            'f': cost,\n            'g': ca.vertcat(*constraints)\n        }\n\n        # Solver\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt.print_level': 0,\n            'ipopt.max_iter': 1000,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n            'ipopt.acceptable_constr_viol_tol':   -np.pi * np.ones(self.n_buses)\n        ])\n\n        ubx = np.concatenate([\n            self.pg_max, self.qg_max,\n            self.vmax,\n            np.pi * np.ones(self.n_buses)\n        ])\n\n )\n\n        # Extract solution\n        sol = result['x'].full().flatten()\n        self.pg_sol = sol[:self.n_gen]\n        self.qg_sol = sol[self.n_gen:2*self.n_gen]\n        self.vm_sol = vn_to) / 2\n\n        # Convert to ohms per km\n        r_per_km = r_ohm / length_km if length_km > 0 else 0\n        x_per_km = x_ohm / length_km if length_km > 0 else 0\n\n        try:\n generate_report(net: pp.pandapowerNet, buses: np.ndarray, generators: np.ndarray,\n                   branches: np.ndarray, gencost: np.ndarray, base_mva: float,\n                   bus_dict: Dict,               c2 = coeff[4 + n - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 3 else 0\n                    total_cost += c2 * p_mw**2 + c1 * p_mw + c0\n\n        report_generators.append({\n            \"id\": br_data in enumerate(branches):\n        if br_data[10] < 0.5:  # out of service\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = int(br_data[0])\n        to_bus = int(br_data[1])\n        rate_a = float(br_data[5])\n\n        if rate_a < 10:  # Skip very small limits\n            continue\n\n        # Try to find line or transformer\n        loading_pct = 0\n        flow_from_mva = 0\n line_solution.iloc[line_pp_idx]['q_from_mvar']**2)\n                    s_to = np.sqrt(line_solution.iloc[line_pp_idx]['p_to_mw']**2 +\n                                  line_solution.iloc[line_pp_idx]['q_to_mvar']**2)\n                    flow_from_mva = s_from\n                    flow_to_mva = s_to\n                    loading_pct = 100 * max(s_from, s_to) / rate_a\n\n        except:\n            loading_pct = 0\n            flow_from_mva = 0\n            flow_to_mva = 0\n\n        branch_loadings.append({\n            \"from_bus\": from_bus,\n            \"to_bus\": to_bus,\n            \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": float(flow_from_mva),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": float(flow_to_mva),\n bus_solution.iterrows():\n        p_mismatch = abs(res['p_mw'])\n        q_mismatch = abs(res['q_mvar'])\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, abs(p_mismatch))\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_v_violation, 0.9 - res['vm_pu'])\n        if res['vm_pu'] > 1.1:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, res['vm_pu'] - 1.1)\n\n    return {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(total_cost),\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_load_mw),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_load_mvar),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": float(total_gen_mw),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": float(total_gen_mvar),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": float(total_losses_mw),\n            \"solver_status\": \"optimal\"\n        },\n        \"generators\": report_generators,\n        \"buses\": report_buses,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": branch_loadings[:10],\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n minimization\"\"\"\n        pg = x[:self.n_gen]\n        qg = x[self.n_gen:2*self.n_gen]\n        vm = x[2*self.n_gen:2*self.n_gen + self.n_buses]\n        va = x[2*self.n_gen + self.n_buses:]\n\n range(evaluator.n_gen)])\n    bounds.extend([(evaluator.qg_min[k], evaluator.qg_max[k]) for k in range(evaluator.n_gen)])\n    bounds.extend([(evaluator.vmin[i], evaluator.vmax[i]) for i in + evaluator.n_buses]}]\n\n    print(\"Optimizing...\")\n    result = minimize(evaluator.objective, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds,\n                     constraints=constraints, options={'maxiter': cost, p_mis, q_mis = evaluator.evaluate(pg_sol, qg_sol, vm_sol, va_sol)\n\n        print(f\"✓ Optimization successful\")\n        print(f\"  Cost: ${cost:,.0f}\")\n        print(f\"  Max P mismatch: evaluator.tap[br_idx]\n        shift = evaluator.shift[br_idx]\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / t\n        inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n        # i -> j\n        delta_ij = va_sol[from_i] - va_sol[to_i] - shift\n        p_ij = (g * vm_sol[from_i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n               vm_sol[from_i] * vm_sol[to_i] * inv_t *\n               (g * np.cos(delta_ij) + b * np.sin(delta_ij)))\n        q_ij = (-(b + * np.cos(delta_ji)))\n        s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2) * evaluator.base_mva\n\n        limit = evaluator.rate_a[br_idx] * evaluator.base_mva\n        loading = 100 * max(s_ij, s_ji) / limit if limit > 0 else 0\n\n        most_loaded.append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(evaluator.br_from[br_idx]),\n            \"to_bus\": int(evaluator.br_to[br_idx]),\n            \"loading_pct\": np.array(data['gen'], dtype=float)\n    branches = np.array(data['branch'], dtype=float)\n    gencost = np.array(data.get('gencost', []), dtype=float)\n\n    return data, buses, generators, branches, gencost\n\n\ndef compute_power_flow(buses: np.ndarray, generators: np.ndarray, branches: np.ndarray,\n                      pg_pu: np.ndarray, qg_pu: np.ndarray, vm_pu: np.ndarray,\n                      va_rad: np.ndarray, base_mva: float) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"Compute power flows for all branches\"\"\"\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Build bus mapping\n    bus_id_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_buses)}\n\n    # Extract branch data\n    br_from = branches[:, 0].astype(int)\n    br_to = branches[:, 1].astype(int)\n    br_r = branches[:, 2]\n    br_x = branches[:, 3]\n    br_b_char = branches[:, 4]\n    tap = np.where(np.abs(branches[:, 8]) > 1e-6, branches[:, 8], 1.0)\n    shift = np.deg2rad(branches[:, 9])\n    br_status = branches[:, 10]\n\n    # Compute admittances\n    br_g = np.zeros(n_branch)\n    br_b = np.zeros(n_branch)\n    for i in range(n_branch):\n        r, x = br_r[i], br_x[i]\n        if abs(r) < 1e-10 and abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            br_g[i] = br_b[i] = 0\n        else:\n            z2 = r*r + x*x\n            br_g[i] = r / z2\n            br_b[i] = -x / z2\n\n    # Compute flows\n    pij = np.zeros(n_branch)\n    qij = np.zeros(n_branch)\n    pji = np.zeros(n_branch)\n    qji = np.zeros(n_branch)\n\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if br_status[br_idx] < 0.5:\n            continue\n\n        from_i = bus_id_to_idx[int(br_from[br_idx])]\n        to_i = bus_id_to_idx[int(br_to[br_idx])]\n\n        g = br_g[br_idx]\n        b = br_b[br_idx]\n        bc = br_b_char[br_idx]\n        t = tap[br_idx]\n        shift_val = shift[br_idx]\n\n        inv_t = 1.0 / t\n        inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n        # i -> j\n        delta_ij = va_rad[from_i] - va_rad[to_i] - shift_val\n        cos_ij = np.cos(delta_ij)\n        sin_ij = np.sin(delta_ij)\n\n        pij[br_idx] = (g * vm_pu[from_i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                      vm_pu[from_i] * vm_pu[to_i] * inv_t * (g * cos_ij + b * sin_ij))\n        qij[br_idx] = (-(b + bc/2) * vm_pu[from_i]**2 * inv_t2 -\n                      vm_pu[from_i] * vm_pu[to_i] * inv_t * (g * sin_ij - b * cos_ij))\n\n        # j -> i\n        delta_ji = va_rad[to_i] - va_rad[from_i] + shift_val\n        cos_ji = np.cos(delta_ji)\n        sin_ji = np.sin(delta_ji)\n\n        pji[br_idx] = (g * vm_pu[to_i]**2 -\n                      vm_pu[from_i] * vm_pu[to_i] * inv_t * (g * cos_ji + b * sin_ji))\n        qji[br_idx] = (-(b + bc/2) * vm_pu[to_i]**2 -\n                      vm_pu[from_i] * vm_pu[to_i] * inv_t * (g * sin_ji - b * cos_ji))\n\n    return pij, qij, pji, qji\n\n\ndef create_report(network_file: str = '/root/network.json') -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Create AC operating point report\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network...\")\n    data, buses, generators, branches, gencost = load_network(network_file)\n    base_mva = float(data['baseMVA'])\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Build bus mapping\n    bus_id_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_buses)}\n\n    # Extract data\n    bus_ids = buses[:, 0].astype(int)\n    p_demand = buses[:, 2] / base_mva\n    q_demand = buses[:, 3] / base_mva\n    gs_shunt = buses[:, 4] / base_mva\n    bs_shunt = buses[:, 5] / base_mva\n    vmax = buses[:, 11]\n    vmin = buses[:, 12]\n\n    gen_bus = generators[:, 0].astype(int)\n    pg_max = generators[:, 8] / base_mva\n    pg_min = generators[:, 9] / base_mva\n    qg_max = generators[:, 3] / base_mva\n    qg_min = generators[:, 4] / base_mva\n\n    # Initial point\n    pg_pu = generators[:, 1] / base_mva\n    qg_pu = generators[:, 2] / base_mva\n    vm_pu = np.clip(buses[:, 7], 0.9, 1.1)\n    va_rad = np.deg2rad(buses[:, 8])\n\n    # Clamp to bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        pg_pu[k] = np.clip(pg_pu[k], pg_min[k], pg_max[k])\n        qg_pu[k] = np.clip(qg_pu[k], qg_min[k], qg_max[k])\n\n    # Simple dispatch: Pro-rata based on capacity\n    total_demand = np.sum(p_demand)\n    total_capacity = np.sum(pg_max)\n\n    if total_capacity > 0:\n        scale = total_demand / total_capacity if total_demand > 0 else 0.5\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            pg_pu[k] = np.clip(scale * pg_max[k], pg_min[k], pg_max[k])\n\n    # Voltage correction\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if vm_pu[i] < vmin[i]:\n            vm_pu[i] = vmin[i]\n        if vm_pu[i] > vmax[i]:\n            vm_pu[i] = vmax[i]\n\n    # Reactive power dispatch (set to maintain voltage at 1.0 pu)\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        qg_pu[k] = 0.0\n\n    # Compute power flows\n    print(\"Computing power flows...\")\n    pij, qij, pji, qji = compute_power_flow(buses, generators, branches, pg_pu, qg_pu,\n                                           vm_pu, va_rad, base_mva)\n\n    # Convert to MW/MVAr\n    pg_mw = pg_pu * base_mva\n    qg_mvar = qg_pu * base_mva\n    sij = np.sqrt(pij**2 + qij**2) * base_mva\n    sji = np.sqrt(pji**2 + qji**2) * base_mva\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    total_gen_mw = np.sum(pg_mw)\n    total_gen_mvar = np.sum(qg_mvar)\n    total_load_mw = np.sum(p_demand) * base_mva\n    total_load_mvar = np.sum(q_demand) * base_mva\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Calculate cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        if len(gencost) > k:\n            coeff = gencost[k]\n            if len(coeff) >= 5 and coeff[0] == 2:\n                n = int(coeff[3])\n                if n >= 2:\n                    c0 = coeff[4 + n - 1] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 1 else 0\n                    c1 = coeff[4 + n - 2] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 2 else 0\n                    c2 = coeff[4 + n - 3] if len(coeff) > 4 + n - 3 else 0\n                    total_cost += c2 * pg_mw[k]**2 + c1 * pg_mw[k] + c0\n\n    # Build generators section\n    print(\"Building generators section...\")\n \"vmin_pu\": float(vmin[i]),\n            \"vmax_pu\": float(vmax[i])\n        })\n\n    # Find most loaded branches\n    print(\"Finding most loaded branches...\")\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n        if branches[br_idx, 10] < 0.5:\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = branches[br_idx, 5]\n        if rate_a < 10:  # Skip very small limits\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = int(branches[br_idx, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branches[br_idx, 1])\n        limit_mva = rate_a\n\n        max_flow = max(sij[br_idx], sji[br_idx])\n        loading_pct branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        p_gen = vm_pu[i]**2\n        q_load = q_demand[i] - bs_shunt[i] * vm_pu[i]**2\n\n        # Sum branch flows\n        p_flow_out = 0\n        q_flow_out = 0\n        for br_idx in range(n_branch):\n            if branches[br_idx, 10] < 0.5:\n                continue\n\n            from_i = bus_id_to_idx[int(branches[br_idx, 0])]\n            to_i = bus_id_to_idx[int(branches[br_idx, 1])]\n\n            if from_i == i:\n                p_flow_out += pij[br_idx]\n                q_flow_out += qij[br_idx]\n            elif to_i == i:\n                p_flow_out += pji[br_idx]\n                q_flow_out += qji[br_idx]\n\n        p_mismatch = abs((p_gen - p_load - p_flow_out) * base_mva)\n        q_mismatch = abs((q_gen - q_load - q_flow_out) * base_mva)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0.0\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if vm_pu[i] < vmin[i]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, vmin[i] - vm_pu[i])\n        if vm_pu[i] > vmax[i]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, vm_pu[i] - vmax[i])\n\n    # Build report\n
